===== M0004 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 85-119
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
85 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
86 |                            String sourceString,
87 |                            Config config,
88 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
89 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
90 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
91 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
92 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
93 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
94 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
95 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
96 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
97 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
98 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
99 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
100 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
104 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
105 |     try {
106 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
107 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
108 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
109 |     } finally {
110 |       Context.exit();
111 |     }
112 |     Node root = null;
113 |     if (astRoot != null) {
114 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
115 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
116 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
117 |     }
118 |     return root;
119 |   }

===== M0010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1069-1206
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1069 |   Node parseInputs() {
1070 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1071 | 
1072 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1073 |     // individual file parse trees.
1074 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1075 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1076 |     }
1077 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1078 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1079 |     }
1080 | 
1081 |     // Parse main js sources.
1082 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1083 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1084 | 
1085 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1086 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1087 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1088 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1089 |     }
1090 | 
1091 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1092 | 
1093 |     try {
1094 |       // Parse externs sources.
1095 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1096 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1097 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1098 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1099 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1100 |           return null;
1101 |         }
1102 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1103 |       }
1104 | 
1105 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1106 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1107 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1108 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1109 | 
1110 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1111 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1112 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1113 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1114 |           }
1115 |         }
1116 | 
1117 |         try {
1118 |           inputs =
1119 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1120 |               .manageDependencies(
1121 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1122 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1123 |           report(JSError.make(
1124 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1125 |           return null;
1126 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1127 |           report(JSError.make(
1128 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1129 |           return null;
1130 |         }
1131 |       }
1132 | 
1133 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1134 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1135 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1136 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1137 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1138 |           return null;
1139 |         }
1140 | 
1141 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1142 |         if (n == null) {
1143 |           continue;
1144 |         }
1145 | 
1146 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1147 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1148 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1149 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1150 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1151 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1152 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1153 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1154 | 
1155 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1156 | 
1157 |             externs.add(input);
1158 |             staleInputs = true;
1159 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1160 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1161 |             staleInputs = true;
1162 |           }
1163 |         }
1164 |       }
1165 | 
1166 |       if (staleInputs) {
1167 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1168 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1169 |       }
1170 | 
1171 |       // Build the AST.
1172 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1173 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1174 |         if (n == null) {
1175 |           continue;
1176 |         }
1177 | 
1178 |         if (devMode) {
1179 |           runSanityCheck();
1180 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1181 |             return null;
1182 |           }
1183 |         }
1184 | 
1185 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1186 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1187 | 
1188 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1189 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1190 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1191 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1192 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1193 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1194 |         }
1195 | 
1196 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1197 |       }
1198 | 
1199 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1200 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1201 | 
1202 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1203 |     } finally {
1204 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1205 |     }
1206 |   }

===== M0013 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 653-679
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
653 |   @Override
654 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
655 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
656 |       return false;
657 |     }
658 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
659 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
660 |       return false;
661 |     }
662 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
663 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
664 |         return this == that;
665 |       }
666 |       return false;
667 |     }
668 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
669 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
670 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
671 |       }
672 |       return false;
673 |     }
674 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
675 |       return false;
676 |     }
677 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
678 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
679 |   }

463 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.opToStr(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1648-1693
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts an operator's token value (see {@link Token}) to a string
   * representation.
   *
   * @param operator the operator's token value to convert
   * @return the string representation or {@code null} if the token value is
   * not an operator
   */
Method code:
1648 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
1649 |     switch (operator) {
1650 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";
1651 |       case Token.OR: return "||";
1652 |       case Token.BITXOR: return "^";
1653 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";
1654 |       case Token.BITAND: return "&";
1655 |       case Token.SHEQ: return "===";
1656 |       case Token.EQ: return "==";
1657 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
1658 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
1659 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";
1660 |       case Token.LSH: return "<<";
1661 |       case Token.IN: return "in";
1662 |       case Token.LE: return "<=";
1663 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
1664 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
1665 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";
1666 |       case Token.GE: return ">=";
1667 |       case Token.GT: return ">";
1668 |       case Token.MUL: return "*";
1669 |       case Token.DIV: return "/";
1670 |       case Token.MOD: return "%";
1671 |       case Token.BITNOT: return "~";
1672 |       case Token.ADD: return "+";
1673 |       case Token.SUB: return "-";
1674 |       case Token.POS: return "+";
1675 |       case Token.NEG: return "-";
1676 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return "=";
1677 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR: return "|=";
1678 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR: return "^=";
1679 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND: return "&=";
1680 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH: return "<<=";
1681 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH: return ">>=";
1682 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH: return ">>>=";
1683 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD: return "+=";
1684 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB: return "-=";
1685 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL: return "*=";
1686 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV: return "/=";
1687 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD: return "%=";
1688 |       case Token.VOID: return "void";
1689 |       case Token.TYPEOF: return "typeof";
1690 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF: return "instanceof";
1691 |       default: return null;
1692 |     }
1693 |   }

===== M0022 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.optimizeSubtree(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 56-103
Documentation:
/**
   * Tries apply our various peephole minimizations on the passed in node.
   */
Method code:
56 |   @Override
57 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
58 |   public Node optimizeSubtree(Node node) {
59 |     switch(node.getType()) {
60 |       case Token.RETURN:
61 |         return tryReduceReturn(node);
62 | 
63 |       case Token.NOT:
64 |         tryMinimizeCondition(node.getFirstChild());
65 |         return tryMinimizeNot(node);
66 | 
67 |       case Token.IF:
68 |         tryMinimizeCondition(node.getFirstChild());
69 |         return tryMinimizeIf(node);
70 | 
71 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
72 |         tryMinimizeCondition(node.getFirstChild());
73 |         return node;
74 | 
75 |       case Token.HOOK:
76 |         tryMinimizeCondition(node.getFirstChild());
77 |         return node;
78 | 
79 |       case Token.WHILE:
80 |       case Token.DO:
81 |         tryMinimizeCondition(NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(node));
82 |         return node;
83 | 
84 |       case Token.FOR:
85 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(node)) {
86 |           tryMinimizeCondition(NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(node));
87 |         }
88 |         return node;
89 | 
90 |       case Token.NEW:
91 |         node = tryFoldStandardConstructors(node);
92 |         if (node.getType() != Token.CALL) {
93 |           return node;
94 |         }
95 |         // Fall through on purpose because tryFoldStandardConstructors() may
96 |         // convert a NEW node into a CALL node
97 |       case Token.CALL:
98 |         return tryFoldLiteralConstructor(node);
99 | 
100 |       default:
101 |         return node; //Nothing changed
102 |     }
103 |   }

===== M0031 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 75-697
Documentation:

Method code:
75 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
76 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
77 |       return;
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     int type = n.getType();
81 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
82 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
83 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
84 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
85 | 
86 |     // Handle all binary operators
87 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
88 |       Preconditions.checkState(
89 |           childCount == 2,
90 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
91 |           opstr, childCount);
92 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
93 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
94 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
95 | 
96 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
97 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
98 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
99 | 
100 |       // Handle associativity.
101 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
102 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
103 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
104 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
105 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
106 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
107 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
108 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
109 |       } else {
110 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
111 |       }
112 |       return;
113 |     }
114 | 
115 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
116 | 
117 |     switch (type) {
118 |       case Token.TRY: {
119 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
120 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
121 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
122 | 
123 |         add("try");
124 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
125 | 
126 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
127 |         // isn't a catch block
128 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
129 |         if (catchblock != null) {
130 |           add(catchblock);
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         if (childCount == 3) {
134 |           add("finally");
135 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
136 |         }
137 |         break;
138 |       }
139 | 
140 |       case Token.CATCH:
141 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
142 |         add("catch(");
143 |         add(first);
144 |         add(")");
145 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
146 |         break;
147 | 
148 |       case Token.THROW:
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
150 |         add("throw");
151 |         add(first);
152 | 
153 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
154 |         // parse this.
155 |         cc.endStatement(true);
156 |         break;
157 | 
158 |       case Token.RETURN:
159 |         add("return");
160 |         if (childCount == 1) {
161 |           add(first);
162 |         } else {
163 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
164 |         }
165 |         cc.endStatement();
166 |         break;
167 | 
168 |       case Token.VAR:
169 |         if (first != null) {
170 |           add("var ");
171 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
172 |         }
173 |         break;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
176 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
177 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.NAME:
181 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
182 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
183 |         } else {
184 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
185 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
186 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
187 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
188 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
189 |           } else {
190 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
191 |             // precedence.
192 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
193 |           }
194 |         }
195 |         break;
196 | 
197 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
198 |         add("[");
199 |         addList(first, (int[]) n.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP));
200 |         add("]");
201 |         break;
202 | 
203 |       case Token.LP:
204 |         add("(");
205 |         addList(first);
206 |         add(")");
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.COMMA:
210 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
211 |         addList(first, false, context);
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.NUMBER:
215 |         Preconditions.checkState(
216 |             childCount ==
217 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
218 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0));
219 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
220 |         break;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
223 |       case Token.VOID:
224 |       case Token.NOT:
225 |       case Token.BITNOT:
226 |       case Token.POS:
227 |       case Token.NEG: {
228 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
229 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
230 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
231 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
232 |         break;
233 |       }
234 | 
235 |       case Token.HOOK: {
236 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
237 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
238 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
239 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
240 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
241 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
242 |         addExpr(last, 1);
243 |         break;
244 |       }
245 | 
246 |       case Token.REGEXP:
247 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
248 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
249 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
250 |         }
251 | 
252 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
253 | 
254 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
255 |         if (childCount == 2) {
256 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
257 |         } else {
258 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
259 |           add(regexp);
260 |         }
261 |         break;
262 | 
263 |       case Token.GET_REF:
264 |         add(first);
265 |         break;
266 | 
267 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
268 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
269 |         add(first);
270 |         add(".");
271 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
272 |         break;
273 | 
274 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
275 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
276 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
277 |         }
278 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
279 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
280 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
281 |           add("(");
282 |         }
283 | 
284 |         add("function");
285 |         add(first);
286 | 
287 |         add(first.getNext());
288 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
289 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
290 | 
291 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
292 |           add(")");
293 |         }
294 |         break;
295 | 
296 |       case Token.GET:
297 |       case Token.SET:
298 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
299 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
300 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
301 | 
302 |         // Get methods are unnamed
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
304 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
305 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
306 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
307 |           add("get ");
308 |         } else {
309 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
310 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
311 |           add("set ");
312 |         }
313 | 
314 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
315 |         String name = n.getString();
316 |         Node fn = first;
317 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
318 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
319 | 
320 |         // Add the property name.
321 |         if (TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
322 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
323 |             // unicode escaped.
324 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
325 |           add(name);
326 |         } else {
327 |           add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
328 |         }
329 | 
330 |         add(parameters);
331 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
332 |         break;
333 | 
334 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
335 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
336 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
337 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
338 |         }
339 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
340 |         if (preserveBlock) {
341 |           cc.beginBlock();
342 |         }
343 | 
344 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
345 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
346 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
347 |                 !preserveBlock &&
348 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
349 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
350 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
351 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
352 | 
353 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
354 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
355 |             cc.endStatement();
356 |           }
357 | 
358 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
359 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
360 |           }
361 | 
362 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
363 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
364 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
365 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
366 |           }
367 |         }
368 |         if (preserveBlock) {
369 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
370 |         }
371 |         break;
372 |       }
373 | 
374 |       case Token.FOR:
375 |         if (childCount == 4) {
376 |           add("for(");
377 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
378 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
379 |           } else {
380 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
381 |           }
382 |           add(";");
383 |           add(first.getNext());
384 |           add(";");
385 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
386 |           add(")");
387 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
388 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
389 |         } else {
390 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
391 |           add("for(");
392 |           add(first);
393 |           add("in");
394 |           add(first.getNext());
395 |           add(")");
396 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
397 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
398 |         }
399 |         break;
400 | 
401 |       case Token.DO:
402 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
403 |         add("do");
404 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
405 |         add("while(");
406 |         add(last);
407 |         add(")");
408 |         cc.endStatement();
409 |         break;
410 | 
411 |       case Token.WHILE:
412 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
413 |         add("while(");
414 |         add(first);
415 |         add(")");
416 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         break;
419 | 
420 |       case Token.EMPTY:
421 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
422 |         break;
423 | 
424 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
425 |         Preconditions.checkState(
426 |             childCount == 2,
427 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
428 |         Preconditions.checkState(
429 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
430 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
431 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
432 |         if (needsParens) {
433 |           add("(");
434 |         }
435 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
436 |         if (needsParens) {
437 |           add(")");
438 |         }
439 |         add(".");
440 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
441 |         break;
442 |       }
443 | 
444 |       case Token.GETELEM:
445 |         Preconditions.checkState(
446 |             childCount == 2,
447 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
448 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
449 |         add("[");
450 |         add(first.getNext());
451 |         add("]");
452 |         break;
453 | 
454 |       case Token.WITH:
455 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
456 |         add("with(");
457 |         add(first);
458 |         add(")");
459 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
460 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
461 |         break;
462 | 
463 |       case Token.INC:
464 |       case Token.DEC: {
465 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
466 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
467 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
468 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
469 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
470 |         if (postProp != 0) {
471 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
472 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
473 |         } else {
474 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
475 |           add(first);
476 |         }
477 |         break;
478 |       }
479 | 
480 |       case Token.CALL:
481 |         // We have two special cases here:
482 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
483 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
484 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
485 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
486 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
487 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
488 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
489 |         // that must be preserved.
490 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
491 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
492 |           add("(0,");
493 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
494 |           add(")");
495 |         } else {
496 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
497 |         }
498 |         add("(");
499 |         addList(first.getNext());
500 |         add(")");
501 |         break;
502 | 
503 |       case Token.IF:
504 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
505 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
506 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
507 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
508 |           cc.beginBlock();
509 |         }
510 | 
511 |         add("if(");
512 |         add(first);
513 |         add(")");
514 | 
515 |         if (hasElse) {
516 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
517 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
518 |           add("else");
519 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
520 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
521 |         } else {
522 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
523 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
524 |         }
525 | 
526 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
527 |           cc.endBlock();
528 |         }
529 |         break;
530 | 
531 |       case Token.NULL:
532 |       case Token.THIS:
533 |       case Token.FALSE:
534 |       case Token.TRUE:
535 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
536 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
537 |         break;
538 | 
539 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
540 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
541 |         add("continue");
542 |         if (childCount == 1) {
543 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
544 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
545 |           }
546 |           add(" ");
547 |           add(first);
548 |         }
549 |         cc.endStatement();
550 |         break;
551 | 
552 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
553 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
554 |         add("debugger");
555 |         cc.endStatement();
556 |         break;
557 | 
558 |       case Token.BREAK:
559 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
560 |         add("break");
561 |         if (childCount == 1) {
562 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
563 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
564 |           }
565 |           add(" ");
566 |           add(first);
567 |         }
568 |         cc.endStatement();
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
572 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
573 | 
574 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
575 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
576 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
577 |         cc.endStatement();
578 |         break;
579 | 
580 |       case Token.NEW:
581 |         add("new ");
582 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
583 | 
584 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
585 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
586 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
587 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
588 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
589 |         }
590 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
591 | 
592 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
593 |         Node next = first.getNext();
594 |         if (next != null) {
595 |           add("(");
596 |           addList(next);
597 |           add(")");
598 |         }
599 |         break;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.STRING:
602 |         if (childCount !=
603 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
604 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
605 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
606 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
607 |         }
608 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
609 |         break;
610 | 
611 |       case Token.DELPROP:
612 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
613 |         add("delete ");
614 |         add(first);
615 |         break;
616 | 
617 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
618 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
619 |         if (needsParens) {
620 |           add("(");
621 |         }
622 |         add("{");
623 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
624 |           if (c != first) {
625 |             cc.listSeparator();
626 |           }
627 | 
628 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
629 |             add(c);
630 |           } else {
631 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
632 |             // not JavaScript keywords
633 |             if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
634 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
635 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
636 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
637 |                 // unicode escaped.
638 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
639 |               add(c.getString());
640 |             } else {
641 |               addExpr(c, 1);
642 |             }
643 |             add(":");
644 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
645 |           }
646 |         }
647 |         add("}");
648 |         if (needsParens) {
649 |           add(")");
650 |         }
651 |         break;
652 |       }
653 | 
654 |       case Token.SWITCH:
655 |         add("switch(");
656 |         add(first);
657 |         add(")");
658 |         cc.beginBlock();
659 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
660 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
661 |         break;
662 | 
663 |       case Token.CASE:
664 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
665 |         add("case ");
666 |         add(first);
667 |         addCaseBody(last);
668 |         break;
669 | 
670 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
671 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
672 |         add("default");
673 |         addCaseBody(first);
674 |         break;
675 | 
676 |       case Token.LABEL:
677 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
678 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
679 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
680 |         }
681 |         add(first);
682 |         add(":");
683 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
684 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
685 |         break;
686 | 
687 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
688 |       // ignored for our purposes.
689 |       case Token.SETNAME:
690 |         break;
691 | 
692 |       default:
693 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
694 |     }
695 | 
696 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
697 |   }

===== M0037 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Token.name(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Token.java
Original range: 268-440
Documentation:

Method code:
268 |   public static String name(int token)
269 |     {
270 |         if (!printNames) {
271 |             return String.valueOf(token);
272 |         }
273 |         switch (token) {
274 |           case ERROR:           return "ERROR";
275 |           case EOF:             return "EOF";
276 |           case EOL:             return "EOL";
277 |           case ENTERWITH:       return "ENTERWITH";
278 |           case LEAVEWITH:       return "LEAVEWITH";
279 |           case RETURN:          return "RETURN";
280 |           case GOTO:            return "GOTO";
281 |           case IFEQ:            return "IFEQ";
282 |           case IFNE:            return "IFNE";
283 |           case SETNAME:         return "SETNAME";
284 |           case BITOR:           return "BITOR";
285 |           case BITXOR:          return "BITXOR";
286 |           case BITAND:          return "BITAND";
287 |           case EQ:              return "EQ";
288 |           case NE:              return "NE";
289 |           case LT:              return "LT";
290 |           case LE:              return "LE";
291 |           case GT:              return "GT";
292 |           case GE:              return "GE";
293 |           case LSH:             return "LSH";
294 |           case RSH:             return "RSH";
295 |           case URSH:            return "URSH";
296 |           case ADD:             return "ADD";
297 |           case SUB:             return "SUB";
298 |           case MUL:             return "MUL";
299 |           case DIV:             return "DIV";
300 |           case MOD:             return "MOD";
301 |           case NOT:             return "NOT";
302 |           case BITNOT:          return "BITNOT";
303 |           case POS:             return "POS";
304 |           case NEG:             return "NEG";
305 |           case NEW:             return "NEW";
306 |           case DELPROP:         return "DELPROP";
307 |           case TYPEOF:          return "TYPEOF";
308 |           case GETPROP:         return "GETPROP";
309 |           case SETPROP:         return "SETPROP";
310 |           case GETELEM:         return "GETELEM";
311 |           case SETELEM:         return "SETELEM";
312 |           case CALL:            return "CALL";
313 |           case NAME:            return "NAME";
314 |           case LABEL_NAME:      return "LABEL_NAME";

===== M0039 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1865-1879
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1865 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1866 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1867 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1868 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1869 |       n2clone.parent = result;
1870 |       if (result.last != null) {
1871 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
1872 |       }
1873 |       if (result.first == null) {
1874 |         result.first = n2clone;
1875 |       }
1876 |       result.last = n2clone;
1877 |     }
1878 |     return result;
1879 |   }

Method code:
158 |   private Node transformBlock(AstNode node) {
159 |     Node irNode = transform(node);
160 |     if (irNode.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
161 |       if (irNode.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
162 |         irNode.setType(Token.BLOCK);
163 |         irNode.setWasEmptyNode(true);
164 |       } else {
165 |         Node newBlock = newNode(Token.BLOCK, irNode);
166 |         newBlock.setLineno(irNode.getLineno());
167 |         newBlock.setCharno(irNode.getCharno());
168 |         irNode = newBlock;
169 |       }
170 |     }
171 |     return irNode;
172 |   }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)

Original range: 420-429
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
420 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
421 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
422 |          current != null;
423 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
424 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
425 |         return true;
426 |       }
427 |     }
428 |     return false;
429 |   }

===== M0048 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 923-1039
Documentation:

Method code:
923 |   private void toString(
924 |       StringBuilder sb,
925 |       boolean printSource,
926 |       boolean printAnnotations,
927 |       boolean printType) {
928 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
929 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
930 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
931 |         sb.append(' ');
932 |         sb.append(getString());
933 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
934 |         sb.append(' ');
935 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
936 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
937 |         // toStringTree.
938 |         if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
939 |           sb.append(first.getString());
940 |         }
941 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
942 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
943 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
944 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
945 |           sb.append(' ');
946 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
947 |         }
948 |         if (printSource) {
949 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
950 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
951 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
952 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
953 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
954 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
955 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
956 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
957 |           sb.append(']');
958 |         }
959 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
960 |         sb.append(' ');
961 |         sb.append(getDouble());
962 |       }
963 |       if (printSource) {
964 |         int lineno = getLineno();
965 |         if (lineno != -1) {
966 |           sb.append(' ');
967 |           sb.append(lineno);
968 |         }
969 |       }
970 | 
971 |       if (printAnnotations) {
972 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
973 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
974 |           int type = keys[i];
975 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
976 |           sb.append(" [");
977 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
978 |           sb.append(": ");
979 |           String value;
980 |           switch (type) {
981 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
982 |               value = "target block property";
983 |               break;
984 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
985 |               value = "last local block";
986 |               break;
987 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
988 |               switch (x.intValue) {
989 |                 case BOTH:
990 |                   value = "both";
991 |                   break;
992 |                 case RIGHT:
993 |                   value = "right";
994 |                   break;
995 |                 case LEFT:
996 |                   value = "left";
997 |                   break;
998 |                 default:
999 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1000 |               }
1001 |               break;
1002 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1003 |               switch (x.intValue) {
1004 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1005 |                   value = "eval";
1006 |                   break;
1007 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1008 |                   value = "with";
1009 |                   break;
1010 |                 default:
1011 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1012 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1013 |               }
1014 |               break;
1015 |             default:
1016 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
1017 |               if (obj != null) {
1018 |                 value = obj.toString();
1019 |               } else {
1020 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
1021 |               }
1022 |               break;
1023 |           }
1024 |           sb.append(value);
1025 |           sb.append(']');
1026 |         }
1027 |       }
1028 | 
1029 |       if (printType) {
1030 |         if (jsType != null) {
1031 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1032 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1033 |             sb.append(" : ");
1034 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1035 |           }
1036 |         }
1037 |       }
1038 |     }
1039 |   }

/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
65 |   private String replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
66 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);

===== M0059 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(Node)

/**
   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.
   */
Method code:
2353 |   static JSDocInfo getFunctionInfo(Node n) {
2354 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
2355 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2356 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2357 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2358 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2359 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
2360 |         // on ASSIGNs
2361 |         fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2362 |       } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
2363 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2364 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2365 |       }
2366 |     }
2367 |     return fnInfo;
2368 |   }

===== M0065 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

===== M0068 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1404-1427
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1404 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1405 |     boolean eq = false;
1406 | 
1407 |     if (type == node2.getType() && getChildCount() == node2.getChildCount()
1408 |         && getNodeClass(this) == getNodeClass(node2)) {
1409 |       eq = this.isEquivalentTo(node2);
1410 |     }
1411 | 
1412 |     if (!eq) {
1413 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1414 |     }
1415 | 
1416 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1417 |     Node n, n2;
1418 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1419 |          res == null && n != null;
1420 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1421 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1422 |       if (res != null) {
1423 |         return res;
1424 |       }
1425 |     }
1426 |     return res;
1427 |   }

===== M0079 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 502-552
Documentation:

Method code:
502 |    @Override
503 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
504 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
505 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
506 |       if (name == null) {
507 |         name = new Name();
508 |         name.setIdentifier("");
509 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
510 |       }
511 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
512 |       Node newName = transform(name);
513 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
514 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
515 |         // declaration.
516 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
517 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
518 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
519 |         // same line as the function keyword.
520 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
521 |             functionNode.getLp();
522 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
523 |       }
524 | 
525 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
526 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
527 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
528 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
529 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
530 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
531 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
532 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
533 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
534 |       if (fnName != null) {
535 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
536 |       } else {
537 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
538 |       }
539 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
540 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
541 | 
542 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
543 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
544 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
545 |       }
546 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
547 | 
548 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
549 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
550 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
551 |      return node;
552 |     }

===== M0090 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 108-141
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the boolean value of a node that represents a literal. This method
   * effectively emulates the <code>Boolean()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
108 |   static TernaryValue getBooleanValue(Node n) {
109 |     switch (n.getType()) {
110 |       case Token.STRING:
111 |         return TernaryValue.forBoolean(n.getString().length() > 0);
112 | 
113 |       case Token.NUMBER:
114 |         return TernaryValue.forBoolean(n.getDouble() != 0);
115 | 
116 |       case Token.NULL:
117 |       case Token.FALSE:
118 |       case Token.VOID:
119 |         return TernaryValue.FALSE;
120 | 
121 |       case Token.NAME:
122 |         String name = n.getString();
123 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
124 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
125 |           // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
126 |           // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
127 |           return TernaryValue.FALSE;
128 |         } else if ("Infinity".equals(name)) {
129 |           return TernaryValue.TRUE;
130 |         }
131 |         break;
132 | 
133 |       case Token.TRUE:
134 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
135 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
136 |       case Token.REGEXP:
137 |         return TernaryValue.TRUE;
138 |     }
139 | 
140 |     return TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
141 |   }

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1515-1517
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1515 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1516 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1517 |   }

===== M0107 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 361-376
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
361 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
362 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
363 |       Set<String> directives = null;
364 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
365 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
366 |         if (directives == null) {
367 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
368 |         } else {
369 |           directives.add(directive);
370 |         }
371 |       }
372 | 
373 |       if (directives != null) {
374 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
375 |       }
376 |     }

     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.

Method code:
456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
457 |     {

460 |

466 |

470 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {

473 |         }

478 |          * single 's.
479 |          */

481 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
482 |         return formatter.format(arguments);

58 |       addGuards(Lists.reverse(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards));
59 |     } else {
60 |       int index = Collections.binarySearch(this.guards, guard, guardComparator);
61 |       if (index < 0) {
62 |         index = -index - 1;
63 |       }
64 |       this.guards.add(index, guard);
65 |     }
66 |   }

===== M0142 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1950-1960
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
1950 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
1951 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
1952 |     int len = s.length();
1953 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
1954 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
1955 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
1956 |         return false;
1957 |       }
1958 |     }
1959 |     return true;
1960 |   }

/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
240 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
241 |            arg != null;
242 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
243 |         String argName = arg.getString();
244 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
245 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
246 | 
247 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
248 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
249 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
250 |         }
251 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
252 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
253 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
254 |         }
255 |       }
256 |     }

   */

694 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {

===== M0186 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1151-1162
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1151 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1152 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1153 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1154 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1155 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1156 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1157 |       } else {
1158 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1159 |       }
1160 |     }
1161 |     return builder.build();
1162 |   }

===== M0193 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 175-191
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }
191 |     }

===== M0198 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isStatement(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java

/**
   * @return Whether the node is used as a statement.
   */
Method code:
1326 |   static boolean isStatement(Node n) {
1327 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1328 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1329 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1330 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.
1331 |     Preconditions.checkState(parent != null);
1332 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1333 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1334 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1335 |       case Token.LABEL:
1336 |         return true;
1337 |       default:
1338 |         return false;
1339 |     }
1340 |   }

===== M0204 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 775-789
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
776 |     if (item == null) {
777 |       return null;
778 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
779 |       return item.next;
780 |     } else {
781 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);
782 |       if (result != item.next) {
783 |         return new PropListItem(
784 |             item.type, item.intValue, item.objectValue, result);
785 |       } else {
786 |         return item;
787 |       }
788 |     }
789 |   }

===== M0207 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.isFoldableExpressBlock(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 519-538
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the node is a block with a single statement that is
   *     an expression.
   */
Method code:
519 |   private boolean isFoldableExpressBlock(Node n) {
520 |     if (n.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
521 |       if (n.hasOneChild()) {
522 |         Node maybeExpr = n.getFirstChild();
523 |           // IE has a bug where event handlers behave differently when
524 |           // their return value is used vs. when their return value is in
525 |           // an EXPR_RESULT. It's pretty freaking weird. See:
526 |           // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=291
527 |           // We try to detect this case, and not fold EXPR_RESULTs
528 |           // into other expressions.
529 | 
530 |             // We only have to worry about methods with an implicit 'this'
531 |             // param, or this doesn't happen.
532 | 
533 |           return NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(maybeExpr);
534 |       }
535 |     }
536 | 
537 |     return false;
538 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 900-975
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
900 |   static int precedence(int type) {
901 |     switch (type) {
902 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
903 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
904 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
905 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
906 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
907 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
908 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
909 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
910 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
911 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
912 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
913 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
914 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
915 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
916 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
917 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
918 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
919 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
920 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
921 |       case Token.EQ:
922 |       case Token.NE:
923 |       case Token.SHEQ:
924 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
925 |       case Token.LT:
926 |       case Token.GT:
927 |       case Token.LE:
928 |       case Token.GE:
929 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
930 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
931 |       case Token.LSH:
932 |       case Token.RSH:
933 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
934 |       case Token.SUB:
935 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
936 |       case Token.MUL:
937 |       case Token.MOD:
938 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
939 |       case Token.INC:
940 |       case Token.DEC:
941 |       case Token.NEW:
942 |       case Token.DELPROP:
943 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
944 |       case Token.VOID:
945 |       case Token.NOT:
946 |       case Token.BITNOT:
947 |       case Token.POS:
948 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
949 | 
950 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
951 |       case Token.CALL:
952 |       case Token.EMPTY:
953 |       case Token.FALSE:
954 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
955 |       case Token.GETELEM:
956 |       case Token.GETPROP:
957 |       case Token.GET_REF:
958 |       case Token.IF:
959 |       case Token.LP:
960 |       case Token.NAME:
961 |       case Token.NULL:
962 |       case Token.NUMBER:
963 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
964 |       case Token.REGEXP:
965 |       case Token.RETURN:
966 |       case Token.STRING:
967 |       case Token.THIS:
968 |       case Token.TRUE:
969 |         return 15;
970 | 
971 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
972 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
973 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
974 |     }
975 |   }

===== M0225 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1595-1632
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1595 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1596 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1597 |       try {
1598 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1599 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1600 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1601 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1602 |             return false;
1603 |           }
1604 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1605 |           return false;
1606 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1607 |           return false;
1608 |         } else {
1609 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1610 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1611 |               return false;
1612 |             }
1613 |           }
1614 |         }
1615 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1616 |         return false;
1617 |       }
1618 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1619 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1620 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1621 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1622 |         return false;
1623 |       }
1624 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1625 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1626 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1627 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1628 |         return false;
1629 |       }
1630 |     }
1631 |     return true;
1632 |   }

===== M0242 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 451-467
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
451 |     @Override
452 |     void maybeCutLine() {
453 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
454 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
455 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
456 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
457 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
458 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
459 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
460 |           lineIndex++;
461 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
462 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
463 |         } else {
464 |           startNewLine();
465 |         }
466 |       }
467 |     }

   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
598 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
599 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
600 |     if (typeSet == null) {
601 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
602 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
603 |     }
604 | 
605 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
606 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
607 | 
608 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
609 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
610 |   }

===== M0247 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 755-797
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
755 |   @Override
756 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
757 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
758 |       return true;
759 |     }
760 | 
761 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
762 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
763 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
764 |         return true;
765 |       }
766 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
767 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
768 |         return false;
769 |       }
770 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
771 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
772 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
773 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
774 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
775 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
776 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
777 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
778 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
779 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
780 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
781 | 
782 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
783 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
784 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
785 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
786 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
787 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
788 | 
789 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
790 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
791 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
792 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
793 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
794 |     }
795 | 
796 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
797 |   }

===== M0250 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 842-869
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
842 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
843 |     // unknown
844 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
845 |       return true;
846 |     }
847 |     // equality
848 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
849 |       return true;
850 |     }
851 |     // all type
852 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
853 |       return true;
854 |     }
855 |     // unions
856 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
857 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
858 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
859 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
860 |           return true;
861 |         }
862 |       }
863 |     }
864 |     // named types
865 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
866 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
867 |     }
868 |     return false;
869 |   }

===== M0263 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 294-302
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
294 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
295 |       Node ret = transform(n);
296 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
297 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
298 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
299 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
300 |       }
301 |       return ret;
302 |     }

===== M0272 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
842 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
843 |     int count = 0;
844 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.next) {
845 |       count++;
846 |     }
847 | 
848 |     int[] keys = new int[count];
849 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.next) {

852 |     }
853 | 
854 |     Arrays.sort(keys);
855 |     return keys;
856 |   }
