===== M0275 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.addNonEmptyStatement(Node,Context,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 714-770
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds a block or expression, substituting a VOID with an empty statement.
   * This is used for "for (...);" and "if (...);" type statements.
   *
   * @param n The node to print.
   * @param context The context to determine how the node should be printed.
   */
Method code:
714 |   private void addNonEmptyStatement(
715 |       Node n, Context context, boolean allowNonBlockChild) {
716 |     Node nodeToProcess = n;
717 | 
718 |     if (!allowNonBlockChild && n.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
719 |       throw new Error("Missing BLOCK child.");
720 |     }
721 | 
722 |     // Strip unneeded blocks, that is blocks with <2 children unless
723 |     // the CodePrinter specifically wants to keep them.
724 |     if (n.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
725 |       int count = getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2);
726 |       if (count == 0) {
727 |         if (cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks()) {
728 |           cc.beginBlock();
729 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
730 |         } else {
731 |           cc.endStatement(true);
732 |         }
733 |         return;
734 |       }
735 | 
736 |       if (count == 1) {
737 |         // Hack around a couple of browser bugs:
738 |         //   Safari needs a block around function declarations.
739 |         //   IE6/7 needs a block around DOs.
740 |         Node firstAndOnlyChild = getFirstNonEmptyChild(n);
741 |         boolean alwaysWrapInBlock = cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks();
742 |         if (alwaysWrapInBlock || isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(firstAndOnlyChild)) {
743 |           cc.beginBlock();
744 |           add(firstAndOnlyChild, Context.STATEMENT);
745 |           cc.maybeLineBreak();
746 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
747 |           return;
748 |         } else {
749 |           // Continue with the only child.
750 |           nodeToProcess = firstAndOnlyChild;
751 |         }
752 |       }
753 | 
754 |       if (count > 1) {
755 |         context = Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
756 |       }
757 |     }
758 | 
759 |     if (nodeToProcess.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
760 |       cc.endStatement(true);
761 |     } else {
762 |       add(nodeToProcess, context);
763 | 
764 |       // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions - so any
765 |       // VAR in a statement context needs a call to endStatement() here.
766 |       if (nodeToProcess.getType() == Token.VAR) {
767 |         cc.endStatement();
768 |       }
769 |     }
770 |   }

===== M0276 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 401-417
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
401 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
402 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
403 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
404 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
405 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
406 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
407 |         String inputName = input.getName();
408 | 
409 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
410 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
411 |         // will get an error down the line.
412 |         inputs.add(input);
413 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
414 |       }
415 |     }
416 |     return inputs;
417 |   }

===== M0290 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1004-1024
Documentation:

Method code:
1004 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1005 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1006 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1007 |       return s;
1008 |     }
1009 | 
1010 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1011 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1012 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1013 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1014 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1015 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1016 |       // crazy.
1017 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1018 |         sb.append(c);
1019 |       } else {
1020 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1021 |       }
1022 |     }
1023 |     return sb.toString();
1024 |   }

===== M0299 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1146-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

===== M0307 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 408-452
Documentation:

Method code:
408 |   @Override
409 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
410 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
411 |       return true;
412 |     }
413 | 
414 |     // Union types
415 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
416 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
417 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
418 |       return false;
419 |     }
420 | 
421 |     // record types
422 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
423 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Interfaces
427 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
428 |     // to the interface instance.
429 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
430 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
431 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
432 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
433 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
434 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
435 |           return true;
436 |         }
437 |       }
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // other prototype based objects
441 |     if (that != null) {
442 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
443 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
444 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
445 |         // to avoid guessing.
446 |         return true;
447 |       }
448 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
449 |     }
450 | 
451 |     return false;
452 |   }

===== M0317 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.getNonEmptyChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1030-1041
Documentation:
/**
   * @param maxCount The maximum number of children to look for.
   * @return The number of children of this node that are non empty up to
   * maxCount.
   */
Method code:
1030 |   private static int getNonEmptyChildCount(Node n, int maxCount) {
1031 |     int i = 0;
1032 |     Node c = n.getFirstChild();
1033 |     for (; c != null && i < maxCount; c = c.getNext()) {
1034 |       if (c.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
1035 |         i += getNonEmptyChildCount(c, maxCount-i);
1036 |       } else if (c.getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
1037 |         i++;
1038 |       }
1039 |     }
1040 |     return i;
1041 |   }

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.has(Node,Predicate<Node>,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2178-2196
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the predicate is true for the node or any of its children.
   */
Method code:
2178 |   static boolean has(Node node,
2179 |                      Predicate<Node> pred,
2180 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2181 |     if (pred.apply(node)) {
2182 |       return true;
2183 |     }
2184 | 
2185 |     if (!traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2186 |       return false;
2187 |     }
2188 | 
2189 |     for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2190 |       if (has(c, pred, traverseChildrenPred)) {
2191 |         return true;
2192 |       }
2193 |     }

===== M0346 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.tryMinimizeIf(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 258-462
Documentation:
/**
   * Try turning IF nodes into smaller HOOKs
   *
   * Returns the replacement for n or the original if no replacement was
   * necessary.
   */
Method code:
258 |   private Node tryMinimizeIf(Node n) {
259 | 
260 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
261 | 
262 |     Node cond = n.getFirstChild();
263 | 
264 |     /* If the condition is a literal, we'll let other
265 |      * optimizations try to remove useless code.
266 |      */
267 |     if (NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(cond, true)) {
268 |       return n;
269 |     }
270 | 
271 |     Node thenBranch = cond.getNext();
272 |     Node elseBranch = thenBranch.getNext();
273 | 
274 |     if (elseBranch == null) {
275 |       if (isFoldableExpressBlock(thenBranch)) {
276 |         Node expr = getBlockExpression(thenBranch);
277 |         if (isPropertyAssignmentInExpression(expr)) {
278 |           // Keep opportunities for CollapseProperties such as
279 |           // a.longIdentifier || a.longIdentifier = ... -> var a = ...;
280 |           return n;
281 |         }
282 | 
283 |         if (cond.getType() == Token.NOT) {
284 |           // if(!x)bar(); -> x||bar();
285 |           if (isLowerPrecedenceInExpression(cond, OR_PRECEDENCE) &&
286 |               isLowerPrecedenceInExpression(expr.getFirstChild(),
287 |                   OR_PRECEDENCE)) {
288 |             // It's not okay to add two sets of parentheses.
289 |             return n;
290 |           }
291 | 
292 |           Node or = new Node(Token.OR, cond.removeFirstChild(),
293 |           expr.removeFirstChild()).copyInformationFrom(n);
294 |           Node newExpr = NodeUtil.newExpr(or);
295 |           parent.replaceChild(n, newExpr);
296 |           reportCodeChange();
297 | 
298 |           return newExpr;
299 |         }
300 | 
301 |         // if(x)foo(); -> x&&foo();
302 |         if (isLowerPrecedenceInExpression(cond, AND_PRECEDENCE) ||
303 |             isLowerPrecedenceInExpression(expr.getFirstChild(),
304 |                 AND_PRECEDENCE)) {
305 |           // One additional set of parentheses isn't worth it.
306 |           return n;
307 |         }
308 | 
309 |         n.removeChild(cond);
310 |         Node and = new Node(Token.AND, cond, expr.removeFirstChild())
311 |                        .copyInformationFrom(n);
312 |         Node newExpr = NodeUtil.newExpr(and);
313 |         parent.replaceChild(n, newExpr);
314 |         reportCodeChange();
315 | 
316 |         return newExpr;
317 |       }
318 | 
319 |       return n;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     /* TODO(dcc) This modifies the siblings of n, which is undesirable for a
323 |      * peephole optimization. This should probably get moved to another pass.
324 |      */
325 |     tryRemoveRepeatedStatements(n);
326 | 
327 |     // if(!x)foo();else bar(); -> if(x)bar();else foo();
328 |     // An additional set of curly braces isn't worth it.
329 |     if (cond.getType() == Token.NOT && !consumesDanglingElse(elseBranch)) {
330 |       n.replaceChild(cond, cond.removeFirstChild());
331 |       n.removeChild(thenBranch);
332 |       n.addChildToBack(thenBranch);
333 |       reportCodeChange();
334 |       return n;
335 |     }
336 | 
337 |     // if(x)return 1;else return 2; -> return x?1:2;
338 |     if (isReturnExpressBlock(thenBranch) && isReturnExpressBlock(elseBranch)) {
339 |       Node thenExpr = getBlockReturnExpression(thenBranch);
340 |       Node elseExpr = getBlockReturnExpression(elseBranch);
341 |       n.removeChild(cond);
342 |       thenExpr.detachFromParent();
343 |       elseExpr.detachFromParent();
344 | 
345 |       // note - we ignore any cases with "return;", technically this
346 |       // can be converted to "return undefined;" or some variant, but
347 |       // that does not help code size.
348 |       Node hookNode = new Node(Token.HOOK, cond, thenExpr, elseExpr)
349 |                           .copyInformationFrom(n);
350 |       Node returnNode = new Node(Token.RETURN, hookNode);
351 |       parent.replaceChild(n, returnNode);
352 |       reportCodeChange();
353 |       return returnNode;
354 |     }
355 | 
356 |     boolean thenBranchIsExpressionBlock = isFoldableExpressBlock(thenBranch);
357 |     boolean elseBranchIsExpressionBlock = isFoldableExpressBlock(elseBranch);
358 | 
359 |     if (thenBranchIsExpressionBlock && elseBranchIsExpressionBlock) {
360 |       Node thenOp = getBlockExpression(thenBranch).getFirstChild();
361 |       Node elseOp = getBlockExpression(elseBranch).getFirstChild();
362 |       if (thenOp.getType() == elseOp.getType()) {
363 |         // if(x)a=1;else a=2; -> a=x?1:2;
364 |         if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(thenOp)) {
365 |           Node lhs = thenOp.getFirstChild();
366 |           if (areNodesEqualForInlining(lhs, elseOp.getFirstChild()) &&
367 |               // if LHS has side effects, don't proceed [since the optimization
368 |               // evaluates LHS before cond]
369 |               // NOTE - there are some circumstances where we can
370 |               // proceed even if there are side effects...
371 |               !mayEffectMutableState(lhs)) {
372 | 
373 |             n.removeChild(cond);
374 |             Node assignName = thenOp.removeFirstChild();
375 |             Node thenExpr = thenOp.removeFirstChild();
376 |             Node elseExpr = elseOp.getLastChild();
377 |             elseOp.removeChild(elseExpr);
378 | 
379 |             Node hookNode = new Node(Token.HOOK, cond, thenExpr, elseExpr)
380 |                                 .copyInformationFrom(n);
381 |             Node assign = new Node(thenOp.getType(), assignName, hookNode)
382 |                               .copyInformationFrom(thenOp);
383 |             Node expr = NodeUtil.newExpr(assign);
384 |             parent.replaceChild(n, expr);
385 |             reportCodeChange();
386 | 
387 |             return expr;
388 |           }
389 |         } else if (NodeUtil.isCall(thenOp)) {
390 |           // if(x)foo();else bar(); -> x?foo():bar()
391 |           n.removeChild(cond);
392 |           thenOp.detachFromParent();
393 |           elseOp.detachFromParent();
394 |           Node hookNode = new Node(Token.HOOK, cond, thenOp, elseOp)
395 |                               .copyInformationFrom(n);
396 |           Node expr = NodeUtil.newExpr(hookNode);
397 |           parent.replaceChild(n, expr);
398 |           reportCodeChange();
399 | 
400 |           return expr;
401 |         }
402 |       }
403 |       return n;
404 |     }
405 | 
406 |     boolean thenBranchIsVar = isVarBlock(thenBranch);
407 |     boolean elseBranchIsVar = isVarBlock(elseBranch);
408 | 
409 |     // if(x)var y=1;else y=2  ->  var y=x?1:2
410 |     if (thenBranchIsVar && elseBranchIsExpressionBlock &&
411 |         NodeUtil.isAssign(getBlockExpression(elseBranch).getFirstChild())) {
412 | 
413 |       Node var = getBlockVar(thenBranch);
414 |       Node elseAssign = getBlockExpression(elseBranch).getFirstChild();
415 | 
416 |       Node name1 = var.getFirstChild();
417 |       Node maybeName2 = elseAssign.getFirstChild();
418 | 
419 |       if (name1.hasChildren()
420 |           && maybeName2.getType() == Token.NAME
421 |           && name1.getString().equals(maybeName2.getString())) {
422 |         Node thenExpr = name1.removeChildren();
423 |         Node elseExpr = elseAssign.getLastChild().detachFromParent();
424 |         cond.detachFromParent();
425 |         Node hookNode = new Node(Token.HOOK, cond, thenExpr, elseExpr)
426 |                             .copyInformationFrom(n);
427 |         var.detachFromParent();
428 |         name1.addChildrenToBack(hookNode);
429 |         parent.replaceChild(n, var);
430 |         reportCodeChange();
431 |         return var;
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |     // if(x)y=1;else var y=2  ->  var y=x?1:2
435 |     } else if (elseBranchIsVar && thenBranchIsExpressionBlock &&
436 |         NodeUtil.isAssign(getBlockExpression(thenBranch).getFirstChild())) {
437 | 
438 |       Node var = getBlockVar(elseBranch);
439 |       Node thenAssign = getBlockExpression(thenBranch).getFirstChild();
440 | 
441 |       Node maybeName1 = thenAssign.getFirstChild();
442 |       Node name2 = var.getFirstChild();
443 | 
444 |       if (name2.hasChildren()
445 |           && maybeName1.getType() == Token.NAME
446 |           && maybeName1.getString().equals(name2.getString())) {
447 |         Node thenExpr = thenAssign.getLastChild().detachFromParent();
448 |         Node elseExpr = name2.removeChildren();
449 |         cond.detachFromParent();
450 |         Node hookNode = new Node(Token.HOOK, cond, thenExpr, elseExpr)
451 |                             .copyInformationFrom(n);
452 |         var.detachFromParent();
453 |         name2.addChildrenToBack(hookNode);
454 |         parent.replaceChild(n, var);
455 |         reportCodeChange();
456 | 
457 |         return var;
458 |       }
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     return n;
462 |   }

===== M0347 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)

===== M0348 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.tryMinimizeCondition(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 672-809
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to minimize conditions expressions, as there are additional
   * assumptions that can be made when it is known that the final result
   * is a boolean.
   *
   * The following transformations are done recursively:
   *   !(x||y) --> !x&&!y
   *   !(x&&y) --> !x||!y
   *   !!x     --> x
   * Thus:
   *   !(x&&!y) --> !x||!!y --> !x||y
   *
   *   Returns the replacement for n, or the original if no change was made
   */
Method code:
672 |   private Node tryMinimizeCondition(Node n) {
673 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
674 | 
675 |     switch (n.getType()) {
676 |       case Token.NOT:
677 |         Node first = n.getFirstChild();
678 |         switch (first.getType()) {
679 |           case Token.NOT: {
680 |               Node newRoot = first.removeFirstChild();
681 |               parent.replaceChild(n, newRoot);
682 |               reportCodeChange();
683 |               // No need to traverse, tryMinimizeCondition is called on the
684 |               // NOT children are handled below.
685 |               return newRoot;
686 |             }
687 |           case Token.AND:
688 |           case Token.OR: {
689 |               Node leftParent = first.getFirstChild();
690 |               Node rightParent = first.getLastChild();
691 |               if (leftParent.getType() == Token.NOT
692 |                   && rightParent.getType() == Token.NOT) {
693 |                 Node left = leftParent.removeFirstChild();
694 |                 Node right = rightParent.removeFirstChild();
695 | 
696 |                 int newOp = (first.getType() == Token.AND) ? Token.OR : Token.AND;
697 |                 Node newRoot = new Node(newOp, left, right);
698 |                 parent.replaceChild(n, newRoot);
699 |                 reportCodeChange();
700 |                 // No need to traverse, tryMinimizeCondition is called on the
701 |                 // AND and OR children below.
702 |                 return newRoot;
703 |               }
704 |             }
705 |             break;
706 |         }
707 |         // No need to traverse, tryMinimizeCondition is called on the NOT
708 |         // children in the general case in the main post-order traversal.
709 |         return n;
710 | 
711 |       case Token.OR:
712 |       case Token.AND: {
713 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
714 |         Node right = n.getLastChild();
715 | 
716 |         // Because the expression is in a boolean context minimize
717 |         // the children, this can't be done in the general case.
718 |         left = tryMinimizeCondition(left);
719 |         right = tryMinimizeCondition(right);
720 | 
721 |         // Remove useless conditionals
722 |         // Handle four cases:
723 |         //   x || false --> x
724 |         //   x || true  --> true
725 |         //   x && true --> x
726 |         //   x && false  --> false
727 |         TernaryValue rightVal = NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(right);
728 |         if (NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(right) != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
729 |           int type = n.getType();
730 |           Node replacement = null;
731 |           boolean rval = rightVal.toBoolean(true);
732 | 
733 |           // (x || FALSE) => x
734 |           // (x && TRUE) => x
735 |           if (type == Token.OR && !rval ||
736 |               type == Token.AND && rval) {
737 |             replacement = left;
738 |           } else if (!mayHaveSideEffects(left)) {
739 |             replacement = right;
740 |           }
741 | 
742 |           if (replacement != null) {
743 |             n.detachChildren();
744 |             parent.replaceChild(n, replacement);
745 |             reportCodeChange();
746 |             return replacement;
747 |           }
748 |         }
749 |         return n;
750 |       }
751 | 
752 |       case Token.HOOK: {
753 |         Node condition = n.getFirstChild();
754 |         Node trueNode = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
755 |         Node falseNode = n.getLastChild();
756 | 
757 |         // Because the expression is in a boolean context minimize
758 |         // the result children, this can't be done in the general case.
759 |         // The condition is handled in the general case in #optimizeSubtree
760 |         trueNode = tryMinimizeCondition(trueNode);
761 |         falseNode = tryMinimizeCondition(falseNode);
762 | 
763 |         // Handle four cases:
764 |         //   x ? true : false --> x
765 |         //   x ? false : true --> !x
766 |         //   x ? true : y     --> x || y
767 |         //   x ? y : false    --> x && y
768 |         Node replacement = null;
769 |         if (NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(trueNode) == TernaryValue.TRUE
770 |             && NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(falseNode) == TernaryValue.FALSE) {
771 |           // Remove useless conditionals, keep the condition
772 |           condition.detachFromParent();
773 |           replacement = condition;
774 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(trueNode) == TernaryValue.FALSE
775 |             && NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(falseNode) == TernaryValue.TRUE) {
776 |           // Remove useless conditionals, keep the condition
777 |           condition.detachFromParent();
778 |           replacement = new Node(Token.NOT, condition);
779 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(trueNode) == TernaryValue.TRUE) {
780 |           // Remove useless true case.
781 |           n.detachChildren();
782 |           replacement = new Node(Token.OR, condition, falseNode);
783 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(falseNode) == TernaryValue.FALSE) {
784 |           // Remove useless false case
785 |           n.detachChildren();
786 |           replacement = new Node(Token.AND, condition, trueNode);
787 |         }
788 | 
789 |         if (replacement != null) {
790 |           parent.replaceChild(n, replacement);
791 |           n = replacement;
792 |           reportCodeChange();
793 |         }
794 | 
795 |         return n;
796 |       }
797 | 
798 |       default:
799 |         // while(true) --> while(1)
800 |         TernaryValue nVal = NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(n);
801 |         if (nVal != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
802 |           boolean result = nVal.toBoolean(true);
803 |           int equivalentResult = result ? 1 : 0;
804 |           return maybeReplaceChildWithNumber(n, parent, equivalentResult);
805 |         }
806 |         // We can't do anything else currently.
807 |         return n;
808 |     }
809 |   }

===== M0349 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Casts this to an ObjectType, or returns null if this is not an ObjectType.
   *
   * Does not change the underlying JS type. If you want to simulate JS
   * autoboxing or dereferencing, you should use autoboxesTo() or dereference().
   * Those methods may change the underlying JS type.
   */
Method code:
469 |   public ObjectType toObjectType() {
470 |     return this instanceof ObjectType ? (ObjectType) this : null;
471 |   }

===== M0375 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.defineProperty(String,JSType,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 426-443
Documentation:

Method code:
426 |   @Override
427 |   boolean defineProperty(String name, JSType type,
428 |       boolean inferred, boolean inExterns) {
429 |     if ("prototype".equals(name)) {
430 |       ObjectType objType = type.toObjectType();
431 |       if (objType != null) {
432 |         if (objType.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
433 |           return true;
434 |         }
435 |         return setPrototype(
436 |             new FunctionPrototypeType(
437 |                 registry, this, objType, isNativeObjectType()));
438 |       } else {
439 |         return false;
440 |       }
441 |     }
442 |     return super.defineProperty(name, type, inferred, inExterns);
443 |   }

===== M0377 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

514 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE, U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
515 |     registerNativeType(
516 |         JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE, U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
517 |

519 |     U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE.setImplicitPrototype(FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE);
520 | 
521 |     // least function type, i.e. (All...) -> NoType
522 |     FunctionType LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE =
523 |         createFunctionType(NO_TYPE, true, ALL_TYPE);
524 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE, LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
525 |

527 |     ObjectType GLOBAL_THIS = createObjectType("global this", null,
528 |         UNKNOWN_TYPE /* to be resolved later */);

===== M0391 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 420-446
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
420 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
421 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
422 | 
423 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
424 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
425 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
426 |       return 0;
427 |     }
428 | 
429 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
430 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
431 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
432 |       // a new statistic may have been added
433 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
434 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
435 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
436 |       }
437 |     }
438 | 
439 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
440 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
441 |       return 0;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
445 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
446 |   }

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

===== M0398 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 250-293
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
250 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
251 |     this.type = type;
252 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
253 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
254 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
255 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
256 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
257 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
258 |       int i = 0;
259 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
260 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
261 |         i++;
262 |       }
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
266 | 
267 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
268 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
269 |       return;
270 |     }
271 | 
272 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
273 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
274 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
275 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
276 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
277 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
278 |       trace.truncateEvents();
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
282 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
283 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
284 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
285 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
286 |                     + "try/finally block. "
287 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
288 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
289 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     trace.startEvent(this);
293 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeOptimizationsPass.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeOptimizationsPass.java
Original range: 63-85
Documentation:

Method code:
63 |   @Override
64 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
65 |     Node currentVersionOfNode = n;
66 |     boolean somethingChanged = false;
67 | 
68 |     do {
69 |       somethingChanged = false;
70 |       for (AbstractPeepholeOptimization optimization : peepholeOptimizations) {
71 |         Node newVersionOfNode =
72 |             optimization.optimizeSubtree(currentVersionOfNode);

75 |           somethingChanged = true;
76 | 
77 |           currentVersionOfNode = newVersionOfNode;
78 |         }
79 | 
80 |         if (currentVersionOfNode == null) {
81 |           return;
82 |         }
83 |       }
84 |     } while(somethingChanged);
85 |   }

===== M0415 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 204-228
Documentation:

Method code:
204 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
205 |     JSDocInfo jsDocInfo = handleJsDoc(node);
206 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
207 |     if (jsDocInfo != null) {
208 |       irNode.setJSDocInfo(jsDocInfo);
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
212 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
213 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
214 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
215 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
216 |     } else {
217 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
218 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
219 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
220 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
221 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
222 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
223 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
224 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
225 |       }
226 |     }
227 |     return irNode;
228 |   }

===== M0434 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 337-366
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is a literal value. We define a literal value
   * as any node that evaluates to the same thing regardless of when or
   * where it is evaluated. So /xyz/ and [3, 5] are literals, but
   * the name a is not.
   *
   * Function literals do not meet this definition, because they
   * lexically capture variables. For example, if you have
   * <code>
   * function() { return a; }
   * </code>
   * If it is evaluated in a different scope, then it
   * captures a different variable. Even if the function did not read
   * any captured vairables directly, it would still fail this definition,
   * because it affects the lifecycle of variables in the enclosing scope.
   *
   * However, a function literal with respect to a particular scope is
   * a literal.
   *
   * @param includeFunctions If true, all function expressions will be
   *     treated as literals.
   */
Method code:
337 |   static boolean isLiteralValue(Node n, boolean includeFunctions) {
338 |     switch (n.getType()) {
339 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
340 |       case Token.REGEXP:
341 |         // Return true only if all children are const.
342 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
343 |              child = child.getNext()) {
344 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
345 |             return false;
346 |           }
347 |         }
348 |         return true;
349 | 
350 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
351 |         // Return true only if all values are const.
352 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
353 |              child = child.getNext()) {
354 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child.getFirstChild(), includeFunctions)) {
355 |             return false;
356 |           }
357 |         }
358 |         return true;
359 | 
360 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
361 |         return includeFunctions && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n);
362 | 
363 |       default:
364 |         return isImmutableValue(n);
365 |     }
366 |   }

===== M0437 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 292-313
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
292 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
293 |     switch (n.getType()) {
294 |       case Token.STRING:
295 |       case Token.NUMBER:
296 |       case Token.NULL:
297 |       case Token.TRUE:
298 |       case Token.FALSE:
299 |         return true;
300 |       case Token.VOID:
301 |       case Token.NEG:
302 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
303 |       case Token.NAME:
304 |         String name = n.getString();
305 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
306 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
307 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
308 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
309 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
310 |     }
311 | 
312 |     return false;
313 |   }

===== M0442 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 776-796
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the Node is a DO or FUNCTION (with or without
   * labels).
   */
Method code:
776 |   private boolean isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(Node n) {
777 |     if (n.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
778 |       Node labeledStatement = n.getLastChild();
779 |       if (labeledStatement.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
780 |         return isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(labeledStatement);
781 |       } else {
782 |         // For labels with block children, we need to ensure that a
783 |         // labeled FUNCTION or DO isn't generated when extraneous BLOCKs
784 |         // are skipped.
785 |         if (getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2) == 1) {
786 |           return isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(getFirstNonEmptyChild(n));
787 |         } else {
788 |           // Either a empty statement or an block with more than one child,
789 |           // way it isn't a FUNCTION or DO.
790 |           return false;
791 |         }
792 |       }
793 |     } else {
794 |       return (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION || n.getType() == Token.DO);
795 |     }
796 |   }

===== M0445 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-150
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
128 |               current.isUnknownType()) {
129 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
130 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
131 |               return this;
132 |             }
133 |           } else {
134 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
135 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
136 |               return this;
137 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
138 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
139 |               it.remove();
140 |             }
141 |           }
142 |         }
143 |         alternates.add(alternate);
144 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
145 |       }
146 |     } else {
147 |       result = null;
148 |     }
149 |     return this;
150 |   }

===== M0446 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 157-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
157 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
158 |     if (isAllType) {
159 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
160 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
161 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
162 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
163 |       } else {
164 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
165 |       }
166 |     } else {
167 |       int size = alternates.size();
168 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
169 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
170 |       } else if (size > 1) {
171 |         return null;
172 |       } else if (size == 1) {
173 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
174 |       } else {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0466 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.tryFoldLiteralConstructor(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 866-914
Documentation:
/**
   * Replaces a new Array or Object node with an object literal, unless the
   * call to Array or Object is to a local function with the same name.
   */
Method code:
866 |   private Node tryFoldLiteralConstructor(Node n) {
867 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.getType() == Token.CALL
868 |         || n.getType() == Token.NEW);
869 | 
870 |     Node constructorNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
871 | 
872 |     Node newLiteralNode = null;
873 | 
874 |     // We require the AST to be normalized to ensure that, say,
875 |     // Object() really refers to the built-in Object constructor
876 |     // and not a user-defined constructor with the same name.
877 | 
878 |     if (isASTNormalized() && Token.NAME == constructorNameNode.getType()) {
879 | 
880 |       String className = constructorNameNode.getString();
881 | 
882 |       if ("RegExp".equals(className)) {
883 |         // "RegExp("boo", "g")" --> /boo/g
884 |         return tryFoldRegularExpressionConstructor(n);
885 |       } else {
886 |         boolean constructorHasArgs = constructorNameNode.getNext() != null;
887 | 
888 |         if ("Object".equals(className) && !constructorHasArgs) {
889 |           // "Object()" --> "{}"
890 |           newLiteralNode = new Node(Token.OBJECTLIT);
891 |         } else if ("Array".equals(className)) {
892 |           // "Array(arg0, arg1, ...)" --> "[arg0, arg1, ...]"
893 |           Node arg0 = constructorNameNode.getNext();
894 |           FoldArrayAction action = isSafeToFoldArrayConstructor(arg0);
895 | 
896 |           if (action == FoldArrayAction.SAFE_TO_FOLD_WITH_ARGS ||
897 |               action == FoldArrayAction.SAFE_TO_FOLD_WITHOUT_ARGS) {
898 |             newLiteralNode = new Node(Token.ARRAYLIT);
899 |             n.removeChildren();
900 |             if (action == FoldArrayAction.SAFE_TO_FOLD_WITH_ARGS) {
901 |               newLiteralNode.addChildrenToFront(arg0);
902 |             }
903 |           }
904 |         }
905 | 
906 |         if (newLiteralNode != null) {
907 |           n.getParent().replaceChild(n, newLiteralNode);
908 |           reportCodeChange();
909 |           return newLiteralNode;
910 |         }
911 |       }
912 |     }
913 |     return n;
914 |   }

===== M0470 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 440-454
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
440 |   @Override
441 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
442 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
443 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
444 |     if (unknown) {
445 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
446 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
447 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
448 |         unknown = false;
449 |       } else {
450 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
451 |       }
452 |     }
453 |     return unknown;
454 |   }
